# Audio Events and ElevenLabs Prompts — Chain Runner

Audio event specifications for the colour-chain runner. Sounds conform to the Infinity Games style guide: bright, musical, tuned glass, soft synth, quiet acoustics, confirming progress without jarring or alarming the player.

## Sound Event Matrix

| Event | When it fires | Feel | Duration (s) | ElevenLabs Prompt |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `lane` | Player shifts left or right (`step` action) | Smooth, swift aerodynamic air whoosh with subtle glassy chime | 0.25 | Soft airy whoosh of speed with delicate glass resonance, smooth aerodynamic swipe, gentle neon glide, quiet high-end chime, clean transient. |
| `edge` | Player attempts to shift beyond the outer lane boundary | Muted, soft rubbery friction or cushioned bumper tap, no harsh buzz | 0.20 | Gentle cushioned thud with a soft rubber rebound, subtle low-frequency dampening, smooth tactile bumper bump, non-punitive and muffled. |
| `jump` | Player initiates a jump over the road (`step` jump) | Weightless upward lift, airy synth chime ascending | 0.35 | Soft upward synth sweep with an airy glass flutter, light aerodynamic launch, weightless ascending tone, delicate melodic swoosh. |
| `land` | Player touches down back onto road surface (`land` event) | Cushioned impact, soft pneumatic contact on smooth glass | 0.25 | Subtle cushioned tap on smooth polished acrylic, soft low-mid thud, quiet air release, gentle clean touchdown with zero harshness. |
| `vault` | Mid-air leap cleanly clears a road barrier (`vault` event) | Rewarding airy harmonic shimmer, sleek acoustic satisfaction | 0.45 | Breezy resonant chime with a soft harmonic tail, smooth high-frequency crystalline whoosh, satisfying effortless leap, warm ambient glow. |
| `collect` | Runner collects a crystal gem (`collect` event; pitch shifts up 1→6) | Pure tuned crystal bell pluck, bright luminous chime | 0.35 | Pure crystalline bell ping, tuned glass mallet strike, warm resonant marimba ping, delicate luminous acoustic chime, bright clean decay. |
| `match` | Cashing in a chain of 3, 4, or 5 matching gems (`match` event) | Ascending lush glass triad chord, musical bloom, positive confirmation | 0.65 | Bright ascending major chord played on tuned glass bells, lush synth chime bloom, sparkling warm harmonics, gentle rewarding musical swell. |
| `match (max: true)` | Reaching full 6-gem chain; auto-burst that pushes shadow back 8m | Grand euphoric glass cascade, sparkling prismatic burst, signature celebratory warmth | 1.20 | Grand resonant crystal chime cascade, euphoric harmonic burst, shimmering glass glockenspiel arpeggio, warm luminous synth pulse, breathtaking sparkling finish. |
| `break` | Incomplete chain of 1–2 gems interrupted or reset by different color | Soft glass ping dissipating into warm reverb, gentle fade without harsh buzz | 0.40 | Soft muted glass tone fading into warm misty reverb, quiet dissolved chime, gentle melancholic whisper, subtle non-punitive release. |
| `stumble (break)` | Slowdown from breaking an incomplete chain (cause: `'break'`) | Soft muffled felt tap with subtle dragging friction, gentle deceleration whoosh | 0.35 | Soft muffled felt tap with subtle dragging friction, gentle deceleration whoosh, quiet dampening of sound, completely non-jarring. |
| `stumble (hit)` | Slowdown from grazing a barrier without vaulting (cause: `'hit'`) | Damped velvet-wrapped mallet tap, subtle low wobble, non-intrusive deceleration | 0.45 | Damped wooden block tap wrapped in soft velvet, quiet air friction, subtle low wobble, non-intrusive muffled bump, gentle deceleration. |
| `setting` | Crossing 900m boundary into a new landscape/palette (`setting` event) | Deep soothing atmospheric wash, resonant singing bowl chime | 1.50 | Deep warm ambient synth pad swell, gentle singing bowl resonance, serene harmonic breeze, spacious peaceful transition tone, soft luminous shimmer. |
| `caught` | Shadow storm reaches runner; run ends (`caught` event) | Low velvety gong decrescendo, quiet dissolving whisper, calm closure | 1.60 | Soft velvet gong decrescendo into deep warm reverb, peaceful closing chime, gentle wind dissipating into darkness, calm contemplative finish. |
| `ambient` (looping bed) | Continuous background audio during active run (optional looping bed) | Hypnotic, calming synth pulse and soft neon wind at speed | 4.00 | Warm rhythmic analog synth pulse with soft airy wind rushing, smooth hypnotic bass drone, subtle ethereal chimes, meditative futuristic runner ambience. |
| `caught_sting` (sting) | Instantaneous impact/fade when run ends | Delicate muffled chime submerged in warm water, gentle stillness | 0.80 | Single delicate crystal chime submerged in warm ambient water, soft muffled tone, calm acoustic bloom fading into absolute quiet. |

## Implementation Notes

1. **`collect` Pitch-Shifting:**
   Claude generates a single base sound from the prompt (tuned glass ping at ~523 Hz / C5). During gameplay, pitch-shift in semitone increments from count 1 through 6 (+0, +2, +4, +5, +7, +9 semitones) to create an organic ascending musical scale.

2. **`match` Layering:**
   One base chord sample. When cashing in chains:
   - 3-chain: standard base volume.
   - 4-chain: playback at +1.5 dB with subtle chorus/reverb extension.
   - 5-chain: playback at +3.0 dB with dual octave sparkle.

3. **`match (max: true)` Burst:**
   The crowning audio moment of the run. Full stereo width, rich sparkle, accompanied by the visible push-back of the shadow storm.

4. **`stumble` (hit vs break):**
   Both keep deceleration non-punitive. `stumble (break)` leans toward a dissolved airy release; `stumble (hit)` provides a slightly deeper, cushioned low-end thud without harsh distortion.

=====

{
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\malvi\\Desktop\\Clean Interview Folder\\design\\specs\\audio.md",
  "content": "# Audio spec — Chroma Rush (Coral Coast)\n\nOne timbre family for the whole game: **sunny marimba, tuned glass bells, steel drum,\nsoft surf**. Everything is in **C major** so the collect pitch ladder (C D E G A C…),\nthe match arpeggios and the music never clash. Friendly, never harsh: mistakes are\nsoft and comic, not punishing.\n\nGenerated 2026-11-07 ~15:48 in ElevenLabs (flow \"Chroma Rush — audio\"), two takes per\nevent. Models: Music v2.5 (instrumental, 96 s), Sound Effects v2 (prompt influence 0.6).\n\n| File (`public/assets/sfx/`) | Engine event → when | Length | Prompt idea |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| `music-run.mp3` | `startRun` → loops during the run, ducks on pause, fades out when caught | 96 s loop | 120 BPM C-major marimba, steel drum, ukulele, shakers, no intro/outro |\n| `surf-loop.mp3` | quietly under the music from the first gesture (start screen too) | 10 s seamless | waves on sand, breeze, distant gulls |\n| `collect.mp3` | `collect` (pitch climbs the C-major pentatonic with the chain count) | ≤0.5 s | glass marimba pluck on C5 |\n| `match.mp3` | `match` (3–5); pitch +2 semitones per extra gem | ≤1 s | rising C-major arpeggio + shimmer |\n| `burst.mp3` | `match` with `max` (6-chain) | ≤1.8 s | glass/glockenspiel cascade + wind + steel-drum hit |\n| `break.mp3` | `break` (1–2 chain lost) | ≤0.7 s | two soft descending muted marimba notes |\n| `hit.mp3` | `stumble {cause:'hit'}` | ≤0.6 s | hollow driftwood thunk + shell clatter |\n| `jump.mp3` | `jump` | ≤0.5 s | airy whoosh + coat flutter + tiny boing |\n| `land.mp3` | `land` | ≤0.5 s | sneakers on sandy boardwalk |\n| `lane.mp3` | `lane` | ≤0.25 s used | quick soft swish |\n| `vault.mp3` | `vault` | ≤0.7 s | whoosh over + two-note glass chime |\n| `unlock.mp3` | `colors` (new colour unlocked) | ≤1.6 s | rising sparkle → glass bell chord |\n| `storm-step.mp3` | `chaser {dir:1}` (storm one step closer) | ≤1.5 s | rolling thunder swell + gust |\n| `caught.mp3` | `caught` | ≤2.2 s | thunderclap + storm whoosh + comic marimba wah-wah |\n| `start.mp3` | run start | ≤1 s | steel drum + marimba flourish up |\n| `best.mp3` | end screen with a new best | ≤2 s | short steel-drum fanfare |\n| `ui.mp3` | buttons (play, again, resume) | ≤0.3 s used | wooden pop + glass tick |\n\n## Processing (ffmpeg)\nLeading silence trimmed to < 10 ms (latency kills feel); mono 44.1 kHz MP3; SFX\nloudness-normalised to about −16 LUFS, music −20 LUFS and surf −28 LUFS so effects sit on top.\n±3 % random pitch per play so repeats don't machine-gun.\n\n## Take choice\nClaude can't listen. Takes were chosen on measured onset delay, true peak and\nloudness spread (see `design/specs/audio-takes.md`). **The owner's ears are the real check:**\nswap a take by copying the other one from `design/assets-src/audio/`.\n"
}
```

<details><summary>Claude thinking</summary>

**Tool call · Bash** (15:49:54)

```json
{
  "command": "cd \"/c/Users/malvi/Desktop/Clean Interview Folder\" && mkdir -p public/assets/sfx && cat > tools/audio-prep.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Measure, trim and normalise generated audio takes, then pick one per event.\n\nUsage: python tools/audio-prep.py <takes_dir> <out_dir> <report.md>\nTakes are named <event>-a.mp3, <event>-b.mp3. For each take: decode to mono 44.1 kHz,\nfind the onset (first sample above -40 dBFS), the RMS loudness of the sounding part and\nthe peak. Reject takes that are near-silent. Pick the take with the earliest onset\n(ties: louder sounding part). Trim to the onset minus 5 ms, apply a gain to reach the\nevent's target RMS, cap peaks at -1 dBFS, fade the last 30 ms, encode MP3 96 kbps mono.\nGame-independent: works for any <event>-<take>.mp3 set.\n\"\"\"\nimport subprocess, sys, glob, os, math\nimport numpy as np\n\nSR = 44100\nTARGET = {'music-run': -20, 'surf-loop': -28, 'collect': -16, 'lane': -24, 'land': -20, 'ui': -20}\nDEFAULT = -17   # RMS dBFS for the sounding part of an SFX\n\ndef decode(p):\n    raw = subprocess.run(['ffmpeg', '-v', 'quiet', '-i', p, '-ac', '1', '-ar', str(SR), '-f', 'f32le', '-'], capture_output=True).stdout\n    return np.frombuffer(raw, np.float32).copy()\n\ndef db(x): return 20 * math.log10(max(x, 1e-9))\n\ndef measure(x):\n    th = 10 ** (-40 / 20)\n    idx = np.nonzero(np.abs(x) > th)[0]\n    if len(idx) == 0: return None\n    on = idx[0]; end = idx[-1]\n    body = x[on:end + 1]\n    return {'onset_ms': on / SR * 1000, 'rms': db(float(np.sqrt(np.mean(body ** 2)))), 'peak': db(float(np.abs(x).max())), 'len': (end - on) / SR, 'on': on}\n\ndef main(src, out, report):\n    events = sorted({os.path.basename(f).rsplit('-', 1)[0] for f in glob.glob(f'{src}/*-?.mp3')})\n    lines = ['# Audio takes — measured choice', '', '| Event | Take | onset ms | sounding s | RMS dB | peak dB | chosen | why |', '| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |']\n    for ev in events:\n        takes = []\n        for f in sorted(glob.glob(f'{src}/{ev}-?.mp3')):\n            x = decode(f); m = measure(x)\n            takes.append((f, x, m))\n        ok = [t for t in takes if t[2] and t[2]['rms'] > -45 and t[2]['len'] > 0.05]\n        loop = ev in ('music-run', 'surf-loop')\n        best = min(ok, key=lambda t: (round(t[2]['onset_ms'] / 15), -t[2]['rms'])) if ok else None\n        for f, x, m in takes:\n            chosen = best is not None and f == best[0]\n            why = 'near-silent' if not m or m['rms'] <= -45 else ('earliest onset' if chosen else 'later onset / quieter')\n            if m: lines.append(f\"| {ev} | {os.path.basename(f)} | {m['onset_ms']:.0f} | {m['len']:.2f} | {m['rms']:.1f} | {m['peak']:.1f} | {'**yes**' if chosen else ''} | {why} |\")\n        if not best: continue\n        f, x, m = best\n        y = x if loop else x[max(0, m['on'] - int(0.005 * SR)):]\n        gain = 10 ** ((TARGET.get(ev, DEFAULT) - m['rms']) / 20)\n        y = y * gain\n        pk = np.abs(y).max(); cap = 10 ** (-1 / 20)\n        if pk > cap: y = y * (cap / pk)\n        if not loop:\n            n = min(len(y), int(0.03 * SR)); y[-n:] *= np.linspace(1, 0, n)\n        tmp = f'{out}/{ev}.f32'\n        y.astype(np.float32).tofile(tmp)\n        subprocess.run(['ffmpeg', '-v', 'quiet', '-y', '-f', 'f32le', '-ar', str(SR), '-ac', '1', '-i', tmp, '-b:a', '128k' if loop else '96k', f'{out}/{ev}.mp3'])\n        os.remove(tmp)\n    open(report, 'w', encoding='utf8').write('\\n'.join(lines) + '\\n')\n    print('\\n'.join(lines))\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main(*sys.argv[1:4])\nEOF\npython tools/audio-prep.py design/assets-src/audio public/assets/sfx design/specs/audio-takes.md && ls -la public/assets/sfx | awk '{print $5, $9}'",
  "description": "Measure takes properly, pick, trim, normalise and encode",
  "timeout": 300000
}

{
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\malvi\\Desktop\\Clean Interview Folder\\claude antigravity and others\\tasks\\T6.md",
  "content": "# T6 — Jumping carries you over gems (skip a wrong colour)\n- status: todo\n- for: antigravity\n- goal: A runner who is high enough in a jump passes over a gem without collecting it. Jumping becomes the way to dodge a wrong colour and keep the chain. Barrier and gem spacing leave room to land between jumps.\n- may write: public/src/engine.js, public/src/config.js, tests/skip.test.mjs, tests/engine.test.mjs, tests/jump.test.mjs, tests/tutorial.test.mjs, tests/chaser.test.mjs (only tests the new rule breaks)\n- may run: npm test\n- must not touch: design/, public/src/view.js, public/src/main.js, public/src/audio.js, public/src/sprites.js, public/src/style.css, public/index.html, tools/\n- done when: `npm test` passes, including every test under \"Tests\".\n- context: AGENTS.md, authors-raw-notes/2026-11-07-playtest-jump-collects.md, public/src/engine.js, public/src/config.js, tests/jump.test.mjs\n\n## Owner's report\n\"jumping doesnt seem to work because it still collects the item you were trying to jump over, i think this logic error is the reason why jumps werent spaced out correctly either.\"\nRoot cause: in `tick`, a gem in the runner's lane is collected whenever the runner passes its z, at any height. The T3 spec (Claude's) never said otherwise.\n\n## Rules\n1. `CONFIG.gem = { floatM: 0.9, skipHeightM: 1.25 }`. Gems float at `floatM` (the view already draws them there). A runner whose `jumpY >= skipHeightM` at the moment it passes a gem's z **skips** it: the gem is not collected, stays out of the chain, and the engine emits `{ type: 'skip', color, lane, id }`. The gem is removed from `items`.\n   - A full jump (peak about 1.73 m) clears a gem. A tap (peak about 1.0 m) doesn't, so the tap stays the barrier hop.\n   - Skipping isn't a mistake: no break, no stumble, no chaser step.\n2. **Room to land.** After a barrier, the next row in that barrier's lane may hold a gem only if it's at least `minGapM` (the T3 rule at that speed) beyond the barrier. Rows in the other lanes are unchanged. Keep every existing rule: the thread is always reachable, no fully blocked row, the T3 barrier gap, no barriers before `barriersFromM`.\n3. The thread (the guaranteed chain path) must stay collectable on the ground. Never require a jump to keep the thread.\n4. Tutorial: the `jump` step's gems after the barrier start at least `minGapM` past it, so a full jump over the barrier doesn't skip them.\n5. Determinism unchanged.\n\n## Tests (tests/skip.test.mjs)\n- A grounded runner collects a gem in its lane (as now).\n- A runner at full-jump height passing a gem: no `collect`, a `skip` event, chain unchanged, no stumble, `chaserStep` unchanged.\n- A tap (jump + immediate `jumpRelease`) does NOT skip a gem placed at the tap's peak distance.\n- Chain survives a skip: collect ember ×2, skip a tide gem by jumping, collect ember → chain is ember ×3.\n- Over 5 seeds × 3 minutes of generated track at top speed: no gem in a barrier's lane sits closer than `minGapM` after that barrier, and every row still has a ground-collectable thread gem.\n"
}

**Tool call · Bash** (15:56:20)

```json
{
  "command": "cd \"/c/Users/malvi/Desktop/Clean Interview Folder\" && python - <<'EOF'\nfrom PIL import Image, ImageFilter\nimport numpy as np\nwith Image.open('design/assets-src/storm.png') as im: a=np.asarray(im.convert('RGBA')).astype(np.int16).copy()\nr,g,b=a[:,:,0],a[:,:,1],a[:,:,2]\nhi=np.maximum(np.maximum(r,g),b); lo=np.minimum(np.minimum(r,g),b)\ngrey=(hi-lo<28)&(hi>120)\nalpha=np.where(grey,0,255).astype(np.uint8)\nalpha=np.asarray(Image.fromarray(alpha).filter(ImageFilter.MinFilter(3)).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(1.2)))\na[:,:,3]=alpha\nrowfill=(alpha>40).mean(axis=1); top=int(np.nonzero(rowfill>0.04)[0][0])\nout=a[max(0,top-4):].astype(np.uint8)\nprint('top',top,'of',a.shape[0])\nImage.fromarray(out).resize((900,int(out.shape[0]*900/out.shape[1])),Image.LANCZOS).save('public/assets/storm.png',optimize=True)\nprint(Image.open('public/assets/storm.png').size)\nEOF",

## Claude · 15:57:10

**Jumping over gems:** you're right, and the miss is mine. The engine collected any gem in your lane at any height, because the T3 spec I wrote only covered barriers. I've written T6 for Antigravity with these rules:
- A full jump (peaks at about 1.7 m) carries you over a gem without collecting it. That's how you dodge a wrong colour and keep your chain, with no penalty.
- A tap (about 1.0 m) still only clears barriers.
- Gems in a barrier's lane must leave room to land, using the same speed-scaled gap as barriers.
- The guaranteed chain path is always collectable on the ground.

The visual side is ready for it: a coloured ring and sparks under the runner, plus a pitched-up chime when you skip a gem. Antigravity has already picked T6 up.

**Live now (`8391482e`, smoke tests pass locally and live):**
- The frame-4 coast look with the pre-rendered runner.
- Tutorial hints.
- The storm as three steps.
- **Soundtrack:** a 96-second C-major marimba and steel-drum loop, crossfaded so it loops without a seam, under a quiet surf bed.
- **15 sound effects** in one sunny marimba and glass family, each trimmed to start within 5 ms and normalised: collect, match, burst, break, hit, jump, land, lane, vault, new colour, storm step, caught, start, new best and UI. The collect notes climb in the music's key as your chain grows.

**Ready locally, deploying after T6:** the painted storm bank. It rises with each step and flashes with lightning.

**Other worlds:** dropped, as you asked. The renderer still supports them, but it only lists the coast now.

**Things to know:**
- I can't listen to audio. I picked takes on measured start time and loudness, and every take is in `design/assets-src/audio/` if you want to swap one. Your ears are the real check.
- The times on three PLAN.md entries from my last turn were guesses written without checking the clock. I've corrected them and logged the correction.
- Credits today: about 18,500 for images and about 3,000 for audio.
- Nothing has been committed since `3513b55`.

  "content": "# T7 — The tutorial can never get stuck\n- status: todo\n- for: antigravity\n- goal: Whatever a player does in the tutorial (dodges, misses, idles, mashes keys), it always moves forward and hands over to the normal run within a bounded time. No endless run is possible.\n- may write: public/src/engine.js, public/src/config.js, tests/tutorial.test.mjs\n- may run: npm test\n- must not touch: design/, public/src/view.js, public/src/main.js, public/src/audio.js, public/src/sprites.js, public/src/style.css, public/index.html, tools/\n- done when: `npm test` passes, including every test under \"Tests\".\n- context: AGENTS.md, authors-raw-notes/2026-11-07-gems-tutorial-smooth.md, public/src/engine.js (`setupTutorialJump` and the other tutorial setup functions), tests/tutorial.test.mjs\n\n## Owner's report\n\"someone failing the tutorial results in a failed state of the game where you run forever\" (screenshot: stuck on JUMP with an empty road).\nRoot cause (Claude's reading): `setupTutorialJump` puts one barrier in the runner's current lane and waits for `vault` or a hit. A lane change passes it with neither, and nothing respawns it. The `lanes` and `chain` steps have the same shape: they only advance or repeat on specific events.\n\n## Rules\n1. **Jump step:** the tutorial barrier spans **all three lanes** (three barrier items at the same z, tutorial only), so jumping is the only way through. Hitting it repeats the step (as now).\n2. **Any step's pattern passed without completing:** once the runner is more than 6 m past the step's last authored item, respawn that step's pattern ahead (same as a miss).\n3. **Hard limits:** each step gives up after `CONFIG.tutorial.stepMaxMs` (12000) and advances to the next step anyway. The whole tutorial ends (`done`) after `CONFIG.tutorial.maxMs` (45000) at the latest. On `done` everything hands over as today.\n4. The track is never empty for more than 2 s during the tutorial, except the planned `storm` pause.\n5. Keep the event contract: `{type:'tutorial', step}` on every change, `targetLane` for the lanes and chain steps.\n\n## Tests (tests/tutorial.test.mjs)\n- A runner who changes lane at the jump step (dodging) still faces a full-width barrier and can only pass by jumping or hitting it.\n- A runner who does nothing at all reaches `done` within 45 s of game time.\n- A runner who mashes left/right/jump randomly (seeded) reaches `done` within 45 s.\n- A runner who misses the chain pattern sees it respawn (items ahead again within 2 s).\n- Existing tutorial tests still pass.\n"
}

1 - fx.poseT * 0.5 : 1;\n    ctx.drawImage(fr, -w / 2, -h, w, h);\n    // In-between frame: blend the next run frame in by the phase fraction (8 drawn frames read as ~16).\n    if (name.startsWith('run-') && frac > 0.15) {\n      const nf = runnerFrame(`run-${(fi + 1) % 8}`, tint);\n      if (nf) { ctx.globalAlpha = frac * 0.55; ctx.drawImage(nf, -nf.width * scale / 2, -nf.height * scale, nf.width * scale, nf.height * scale); }\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n    // Footfall dust: a small sand puff each time a foot lands (frames 0 and 4 of the cycle).\n    if (name.startsWith('run-') && fi % 4 === 0 && fx.lastFoot !== fi && state.stumbleMs === 0) {\n      fx.lastFoot = fi;\n      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) fx.dust.push({ x: x + (i - 1) * 8 + (fi === 0 ? ## Claude · 16:02:45

Done locally, not live yet:

- **Gems** are now 30% of the runner's on-screen height and float at the engine's 0.9 m. A full jump clears them, which matters now that T6 is in. T6 passes 50/50 tests: a full jump carries you over a gem with no collect and no penalty, and you get a ring, sparks and a chime.
- **Smoother runner**, all in drawing code, so input and the simulation aren't touched:
  - The next run frame is blended in by how far through the step she is, so 8 frames read closer to 16.
  - Run, jump, land and stumble crossfade over 90 ms instead of popping.
  - A lane change leaves two fading ghost copies of her behind.
  - A soft glow in the chain colour sits behind her while a chain is live.
  - Small sand puffs kick up on each footfall.
  
  That's 2–4 extra image draws per frame at most.
- **Tutorial stuck forever:** I found the cause. The jump step put one barrier in your lane, and changing lanes passed it without a vault or a hit, so the step waited forever. I've written T7 for Antigravity:
  - The jump barrier spans all three lanes.
  - Any missed pattern respawns.
  - Hard limits of 12 s per step and 45 s for the whole tutorial, so it can never stall.

Antigravity is already on T7. I'm watching for its report, and then I'll review it, run the tests and smoke test, and deploy everything together.

## Claude · 16:08:14

Everything is live as version `aad73c19`, and smoke tests pass both locally and on the live URL. All 53 engine tests pass, including Antigravity's T7 fix.

**What to check in your playtest:**
- **Tutorial:** clear `localStorage` (or use a private window) to get it again. Try dodging the jump: the barrier now covers all three lanes. Try doing nothing: it should hand over to the normal run within 45 s.
- **Gems** are now a third of the runner's height.
- **Jumping over a gem:** a full jump (hold Space) carries you over it, with a ring and a chime, and your chain stays intact. A tap only clears barriers.
- **Runner smoothness:** blended in-between frames, crossfades between poses, a ghost trail on lane changes, a glow in the chain colour, and sand puffs at each footfall.
- **Storm:** the painted cloud bank rises one step per mistake.

**One slip:** the first deploy of this batch (`5849c16b`) failed its live smoke test, and was live for about 6 minutes before the fix replaced it. On the real network, the road texture could arrive after the code first tried to draw it. Locally it always loads in time, so only the live check caught it. I added a plain-colour fallback and redeployed. It's logged in PLAN.md.
